We call the centralized policy Pandora's Router. We extend this to a decentralized setting, Pandora's Bidder, where specialists independently decide whether to invest in self-assessment before accepting an offered price to claim a query. Experiments across three domains---a standard multi-LLM benchmark, retrieval-augmented specialists, and LLMs with variable inference-time reasoning---show that Pandora's Router matches the routing quality of exhaustive estimation, while querying the expensive estimator far less often. In the decentralized setting, value-of-information reasoning improves allocative efficiency when competing estimates are accurate; when competing estimates are noisy, however, it can increase the strategic specialist's utility at the expense of others.

</details>


### [176] [AI4AI-Bench: Benchmarking LLM Agents in Algorithmic Design for Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20318)
*Yizhe Chi,Wenyi Li,Deyao Hong,Xiaoqiu Wang,Mingju Gao,Kaisen Yang,Bingxiang He,Youjie Zheng,Calvin Xiao,Qinhuai Na*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AI4AI-Bench基准测试，评估AI系统通过重写训练算法实现递归自我改进（RSI）的能力。结果显示当前最强模型仅能达到理论最优值的25%，且多数尝试未真正改变学习机制；增加推理努力可显著提升尝试意愿和得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试主要通过收集数据或调整超参数来获胜，无法隔离评估AI系统设计新训练算法以优化“计算-能力”交换率的能力，即缺乏对递归自我改进可行性的直接衡量。

Method: 构建包含10个冻结研究存储库的AI4AI-Bench，涵盖10类训练算法。代理需在B300上花费4小时重写训练算法，随后从头运行最多12小时，并使用隐藏评估器与原算法进行对比评分。所有指标归一化至0（无信息模型）到1（任务最优）之间。

Result: 在6种系统、29种配置下的平均得分为0.166，最佳系统得分为0.250。大多数提交未改变模型学习方式；少数改变的均分0.226（其余为0.126）。增加推理努力将尝试改变的比例从8%提升至64%，均分从0.094升至0.196。

Conclusion: 当前AI系统在自主设计能显著提升性能的训练算法方面能力有限，尚未接近理论最优值。增强推理能力有助于提高探索意愿并带来性能提升。该基准及数据已公开以支持后续重复测量。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) asks whether an AI system can improve the process that produces AI systems, so that the next system inherits the improvement. That process is the training algorithm: a better objective or update rule improves the compute\mbox{-}capability exchange rate for every subsequent run, including the one that produces the next agent. Whether RSI is feasible therefore turns on whether an agent can design training algorithms. No benchmark isolates that ability: existing suites are won by collecting data or by tuning hyperparameters, and none tells a change to how a run is executed apart from a change to how the model learns. We present AI4AI\mbox{-}Bench, 10 frozen research repositories spanning 10 training algorithm families. In each task, an agent has 4 hours on one B300 to rewrite the training algorithm; its code is then rerun from scratch for up to 12 hours and scored by a fixed evaluator hidden from the agent, against the repository's original algorithm under the same procedure. Because the 10 metrics are incommensurable, every task is mapped onto one scale on which $0$ is an uninformative model, $0.1$ is the algorithm the repository ships, and $1.0$ is the task optimum. Across 29 configurations of 6 systems on all 10 tasks the mean score is $0.166$, and the best system reaches $0.250$: even the strongest closes under a fifth of the distance between the algorithm that was already there and the optimum. The submissions show where that distance went: most never change how the model learns at all, and the minority that do average $0.226$ against $0.126$ for the rest. More reasoning effort mostly buys the willingness to go there, taking that minority from $8\%$ of submissions to $64\%$ and the mean score from $0.094$ to $0.196$. We release the task suite, the evaluators and every scored submission, so that the measurement can be repeated as these systems change.

</details>


### [177] [An Agentic Approach for Active Data Collection, Travel Behavior Modeling, and Weather-Sensitive Demand Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20320)
*Narges Ahmadi,Yubo Jiao,Jônatas Augusto Manzolli,Jiangbo Yu,Luis Miranda-Moreno*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种整合对话式数据收集、结构化数据处理和行为预测的三智能体工作流。通过聊天机器人进行图像增强的陈述偏好调查，结合多项Logit模型、机器学习（随机森林）及9种本地部署的大语言模型（LLM），评估了不同提示策略和视觉上下文对通勤者出行模式预测的影响。结果显示，随机森林（69.6%）与最佳文本零样本LLM（69.9%）性能相当，而引入受访者所见天气图像的视觉配置将准确率提升至71.5%，证实了多模态LLM在行为预测中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的旅行行为研究通常将数字数据收集与预测建模分开开发和评估，缺乏有效的整合机制。本研究旨在探索如何将对话式数据收集、结构化数据处理和基于大语言模型的行为预测协调在一个可审计的多智能体工作流中，以提高预测精度并验证多模态信息的有效性。

Method: 1. **数据收集**：使用聊天机器人实施图像增强的陈述偏好调查，针对五种预设天气场景收集学生通勤者的出行选择（共454个响应-场景观测值）。
2. **基准建模**：使用多项Logit模型分析天气关联；使用逻辑回归和随机森林作为机器学习基准。
3. **LLM评估**：评估9种参数规模在20亿至350亿之间的本地部署大型语言模型。
4. **提示策略**：测试四种零-shot提示条件，并通过角色设定（Persona）、少样本（Few-shot）和基于视觉的配置进行扩展。
5. **多模态集成**：使用受访者看到的相同天气图像作为视觉上下文输入LLM。

Result: 1. **性能对比**：随机森林达到69.6%的五类准确率；最佳纯文本零样本LLM达到69.9%，无需任务特定拟合。
2. **提示效果**：习惯性旅行信息带来最一致的增益；专家框架（Expert framing）通常优于角色扮演（Role-Play）；当缺乏习惯性旅行信息时，角色设定信息最有用。
3. **少样本学习**：少样本提示改善了多个模型的预测能力，且在小数量示例后收益趋于稳定。
4. **多模态优势**：利用视觉上下文的最佳配置实现了71.5%的五类准确率，表明视觉语境为部分模型提供了额外的预测信息。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了对话式调查、结构化数据处理、传统行为建模、机器学习和多模态LLM预测可以在一个可审计的多智能体工作流中有效协同。特别是，视觉上下文能显著提升LLM在复杂情境下的行为预测性能，为未来结合生成式AI与交通行为分析提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Travel behavior research increasingly combines digital data collection with predictive modeling, yet these stages are often developed and evaluated separately. This study proposes a three-agent workflow integrating conversational data collection, structured data processing, and behavioral prediction. A chatbot-administered, image-augmented stated-preference survey collected mode choices from student commuters across five predefined weather scenarios, yielding 454 respondent-scenario observations. Weather-related associations were analyzed using a multinomial logit model, while logistic regression and random forest provided machine-learning benchmarks. Nine locally deployed large language models (LLMs), ranging from 2 to 35 billion parameters, were evaluated across four zero-shot prompt-and-context conditions and extended through persona, few-shot, and vision-based configurations. Random forest achieved 69.6% five-class accuracy, while the best text-only zero-shot LLM reached 69.9% without task-specific fitting. Habitual travel information produced the most consistent gains, Expert framing generally outperformed Role-Play, and persona information was most useful when habitual travel information was unavailable. Few-shot prompting improved prediction for several models, with gains stabilizing after a small number of examples. Using the same weather images shown to respondents, the best vision-based configuration reached 71.5% five-class accuracy, indicating that visual context may provide additional predictive information for selected models. Overall, the study shows how conversational surveys, structured data processing, conventional behavioral modeling, machine learning, and multimodal LLM prediction can be coordinated within an auditable multi-agent workflow.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [178] [Quantization Beyond Uniform Bit Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19388)
*K. S. Sreeramji,Sabyasachi Basu,Ravishankar Krishnaswamy,Kirankumar Shiragur,Yujia Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出一种针对具有Matryoshka属性（MRL）嵌入的可变比特分配框架，通过非均匀分配显著优于传统均匀量化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量化方案通常对嵌入结构不敏感且均匀分配比特，忽略了现代模型生成的嵌入中存在的显著几何结构，导致在低比特 regime 下效率低下。

Method: 提出一个简单可变比特分配框架，将嵌入划分为连续桶并进行非均匀存储分配；结合贪婪策略实例化应用于乘积量化（PQ）和标量量化（SQ）。

Result: 在具有MRL属性的嵌入实验中，非均匀分配在相同存储预算下表现更优；在同等压缩率下，PQ召回率提升最高达8%，SQ提升最高达18%，尤其在低比特区间改善明显。

Conclusion: 该研究为大规模检索系统的结构感知压缩和索引技术提供了新方向，证明了基于几何结构的非均匀量化优势。

Abstract: Quantization is a fundamental technique to handle the growing sizes of embeddings generated by modern models. Existing quantization schemes are largely embedding agnostic and allocate bits uniformly across dimensions. However, recent models produce embeddings with significant geometric structure. In this work, we investigate whether a variable bit allocation scheme can improve quantization quality under a fixed memory budget. We propose a simple variable bit allocation framework that partitions an embedding into contiguous buckets and allocates storage non-uniformly across them. Using a greedy allocation strategy, we instantiate this framework for both Product Quantization (PQ) and Scalar Quantization (SQ). We perform a series of experiments on embeddings known to have the Matryoshka property (MRL), and consistently observe that non-uniform allocations outperform uniform baselines at identical storage budgets. The largest improvements occur in the low-bit regime, where uniform allocation is particularly inefficient for MRL embeddings. At the same compression rates, variable allocation improves recall by up to 8\% for PQ and up to 18\% for SQ. Our results suggest a new direction for structure-aware compression and indexing techniques for large-scale retrieval systems.

</details>


### [179] [CrossQ: Task-Aligned Cross-Token Conditional Quantization for Late Interaction Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19204)
*Rohit Kumar Salla,Manoj Saravanan,Ramya Manasa Amancherla*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出CrossQ，一种针对ColBERT等晚期交互检索器的自适应量化方法。通过在索引时利用轻量级文档上下文条件化token编码，并采用对齐排名的训练目标，在显著降低存储开销的同时保持了高精度的检索效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晚期交互检索器（如ColBERT）虽然质量高，但面临多向量索引体积庞大的问题。传统压缩方法最小化token重建误差，但这不利于排名性能，因为排名关键依赖于保留稀疏“获胜”token的分数。需要一种能平衡存储效率与排序精度的压缩方案。

Method: 引入CrossQ方法：1. 在索引时计算轻量级文档上下文（不存储），用于条件化token编码，从而自适应地提高文档内有效token保真度；2. 使用与排名对齐的目标函数进行训练，以保留候选分数分布并保护难负例间隔（hard-negative margins）。

Result: 1. 在2 B/token下，MRR@10比最强足迹匹配基线提升+0.010，比最强候选匹配系统参考提升+0.012；在BEIR子集上4 B/token下nDCG@10平均提升+0.009。2. 在4 B/token下实现约64x原始token存储缩减（含元数据约61x，保守估计约58x）。3. 在8 B/token下配合轻量微调，保留全精度ColBERT约98%的MRR@10性能。

Conclusion: CrossQ通过结合文档上下文条件化和排名对齐的训练策略，优化了内存受限场景下晚期交互检索的存储-质量权衡，实现了极高的存储压缩率同时维持了接近全精度的检索性能。

Abstract: Late-interaction retrievers like ColBERT achieve high quality but suffer from large multi-vector indices. Standard compression minimizes token reconstruction error, while ranking depends critically on preserving scores of sparse "winner" tokens. We introduce CrossQ, which adaptively improves effective token fidelity within documents by conditioning token codes on lightweight document context computed at indexing time (but not stored). CrossQ is trained with ranking-aligned objectives that preserve candidate score distributions and protect hard-negative margins. At 2 B/token, CrossQ improves MRR@10 by +0.010 over the strongest strictly footprint-matched quantization baseline and by +0.012 over the strongest candidate-matched system reference. On a nine-dataset BEIR subset, CrossQ improves average nDCG@10 by +0.009 at 4 B/token over the strongest candidate-matched system reference. At 4 B/token, CrossQ achieves 64x raw token-storage reduction, approximately 61x including metadata and approximately 58x under conservative padding/alignment accounting. At 8 B/token, CrossQ with light fine-tuning retains approximately 98% of full-precision ColBERT MRR@10, improving the footprint-quality tradeoff for memory-constrained late-interaction retrieval.

</details>


### [180] [HARP: Hierarchical Adaptive Ranking with Preference-Adaptive Fusion for Query-Based CVE Prioritization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19430)
*Haochen Liu,Zhengzhang Chen,Haoyu Wang,Yanchi Liu,Jundong Li,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出HARP框架，利用历史标注案例和知识图谱，在无需显式偏好描述的情况下实现基于自然语言查询的CVE优先级排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有漏洞评分系统假设固定标准，但实际修复优先级依赖隐式的运营偏好场景，且难以用文本明确表达，导致传统方法无法适应动态偏好。

Method: 构建HARP图 grounding 多视图框架：检索漏洞知识图谱证据，通过策略条件化的全局、企业和用户视图对候选项打分，并从支持库中拟合视图融合权重。

Result: 在三种偏好场景及多种基础大模型上的实验表明，HARP优于多个基线模型，验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效处理隐含偏好的漏洞优先级排序问题，提升了复杂场景下的排序性能。

Abstract: Vulnerability prioritization is inherently preference dependent, since the same CVE can receive different remediation priority under different operational preference scenarios. Existing scoring systems and ranking methods typically assume a fixed criterion. In practice, organizations already operate under a preference scenario, but this preference is often implicit and difficult to express as a written prompt instruction, while triage queries usually do not encode it. Past validated triage cases under the current scenario are more readily available. We study query-based CVE prioritization in this setting and propose HARP, a graph-grounded multi-view framework that ranks candidates from a natural-language query together with a support bank of historical labeled examples from the current preference scenario, without requiring an explicit textual summary of that scenario. HARP retrieves evidence from a vulnerability knowledge graph, scores candidates with policy-conditioned global, enterprise, and user views, and fits view-fusion weights from sampled supports. Experiments across three preference scenarios and multiple backbone LLMs show that HARP outperforms multiple baselines, expressing our method's effectiveness.

</details>


### [181] [SSR-GRPO: Integrating Supervision and Semantic IDs into Reinforcement Learning for Dense Retrieval in E-commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19595)
*Guangxin Song,Xing Fang,Mingmin Jin,Jing Wang,Bokang Wang,Zhentao Song,Junjie Bai,Jianbo Zhu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出SSR-GRPO方法，通过双视角相关性评估和硬负样本挖掘解决EBR中复杂语义处理及R-GRPO噪声问题，已在电商平台部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有嵌入检索（EBR）难以处理复杂语义；微调LLM缺乏鲁棒机制；现有R-GRPO因批量采样有限产生噪声候选，且使用相似训练的LLM作为奖励模型导致相关性评估偏差。

Method: 提出SSR-GRPO：1) 构建双视角框架，结合量化学习生成的语义标识符（SIDs）和稠密表示向量生成无偏相关性评分；2) 利用SID层次相似关系挖掘硬负样本，设计掩码函数过滤组内噪声，并构建检索-DPO任务以捕捉细粒度语义区分。

Result: 大量离线和在线实验验证了SSR-GRPO的有效性，该方法已成功部署于大规模电商平台。

Conclusion: SSR-GRPO通过改进相关性评估和样本筛选机制，有效提升了电商搜索中复杂语义的检索性能。

Abstract: Embedding-based retrieval (EBR) is pivotal in e-commerce search but often struggles with complex semantics. While recent methods often fine-tune large language models (LLMs) for representation learning, they typically lack robust mechanisms for handling complex and implicit semantics. While Retrieval-GRPO (R-GRPO) recently introduced reinforcement learning to dense retrieval, it suffers from noisy top-K candidates due to limited batch sampling and biased relevance assessments caused by using similarly trained LLMs as reward models. To tackle these issues, we propose Supervised Retrieval-GRPO with Semantic Identifiers (SSR-GRPO). Specifically, our method first proposes a dual-perspective framework for relevance assessment. It leverages both Semantic Identifiers (SIDs) produced by quantization learning and dense representation vectors to generate more unbiased relevance scores. Furthermore, leveraging the hierarchical similarity relationships of the generated SIDs, we mine a set of hard negative samples that serve two purposes: (1) to design a masking function integrated into R-GRPO, effectively filtering intra-group noisy samples; and (2) to construct a Retrieval-DPO task composed of positive and negative sample pairs, enabling the model to capture fine-grained semantic distinctions from a pair-wise perspective. By integrating these optimization strategies, we propose SSR-GRPO. Extensive offline and online experiments validate SSR-GRPO's effectiveness, and it has been deployed on a large-scale e-commerce platform.

</details>


### [182] [Training-Free LLM-Based Recommendation with Post-LLM Item Refinement Using Collaborative Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19665)
*Kyungho Kim,Sunwoo Kim,Geon Lee,Shinhwan Kang,Sojeong Kim,Liam Collins,Bhuvesh Kumar,Donald Loveland,Kijung Shin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出CoRRe框架，通过后LLM范式将协同过滤信号注入物品表示，解决大语言模型在细粒度推荐中兴趣过于宽泛的问题，无需训练即可实现高性能推荐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型（LLM）生成的用户兴趣过于宽泛，不利于细粒度物品检索；而现有的将协同过滤（CF）信号融入LLM的方法多采用预LLM范式（如候选重排序或提示增强），效果提升有限。

Method: 提出CoRRe框架，采用后LLM范式，将协同过滤信号注入LLM生成的物品表示中。具体而言，利用物品-物品共同购买图优化物品嵌入的方向，并利用物品流行度调整其幅度，随后与LLM生成的用户兴趣进行匹配以进行排序。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的实验表明，CoRRe一致优于现有的免训练方法，并取得了与基于训练的方法相当甚至更优的性能，且无需任何模型训练或任务特定的微调。

Conclusion: CoRRe是一种有效的免训练推荐框架，通过结合LLM的用户理解能力和协同过滤的结构化信号，显著提升了推荐的准确性和效率。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise for training-free recommendation, but LLM-generated user interests are often too broad for fine-grained item retrieval. Existing methods incorporate collaborative filtering (CF) signals in a pre-LLM manner through candidate reranking or prompt augmentation, yielding limited gains. We propose CoRRe, a training-free recommendation framework with a post-LLM paradigm that injects CF signals into LLM-generated item representations, which are later matched with LLM-generated user interests for ranking. Specifically, CoRRe refines the directions of item embeddings using an item-item co-purchase graph and their magnitudes using item popularity. Experiments on real-world datasets show that CoRRe consistently outperforms existing training-free methods and achieves competitive or superior performance compared with training-based methods, without requiring any model training or task-specific fine-tuning.

</details>


### [183] [Do Sequential Recommendation Benchmarks Really Require Higher-Order Sequence Modelling?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19833)
*Aleksandr V. Petrov,Praveen Chandar,Paul N. Bennett,Hugues Bouchard,Mounia Lalmas*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究指出，当前广泛使用的推荐系统基准测试可能无法有效衡量高阶序列建模能力的提升。简单的近期加权配对探针（如SeqRules和PCTM）在许多数据集上表现优于复杂的Transformer模型（如SASRec），表明这些基准测试在评估高阶序列建模增益方面存在局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管顺序推荐器日益采用语言模型架构以捕捉复杂、上下文相关的交互，但尚不清楚常用的基准测试是否真正需要这种建模能力。本研究旨在验证现有基准测试是否能有效区分简单方法与高阶序列建模方法之间的性能差异。

Method: 提出两种简单的近期加权配对探针：Sequential Rules (SeqRules) 和 Probabilistic Collaborative Transition Model (PCTM)，它们不学习高阶序列表示。使用eSASRec的评估协议，在Amazon多个数据集和MovieLens数据集上进行对比实验，将探针与SASRec及其变体进行比较。

Result: 在三个Amazon数据集上，至少一个探针比eSASReproduction高出15-38%，在MovieLens-1M上高出4.4%（但在MovieLens-20M上低27.3%）。在其余四个数据集中，至少一个探针也比采样softmax SASReproduce高出9-28%。

Conclusion: 广泛使用的基准测试不适合衡量高阶序列建模带来的增益。建议通过将Transformer模型与强近期加权配对探针进行比较，来具体测试基准测试是否能有意义地衡量高阶序列建模的收益。

Abstract: Sequential recommenders increasingly use language-model architectures designed to capture complex, context-dependent interactions. Yet it remains unclear whether widely used benchmarks actually require this modelling capacity. We investigate this question using two simple, recency-weighted pairwise probes that do not learn higher-order sequence representations: Sequential Rules (SeqRules) and our Probabilistic Collaborative Transition Model (PCTM). Using the evaluation protocol of eSASRec, at least one probe exceeds our eSASRec reproduction by 15-38% on three Amazon datasets and by 4.4% on MovieLens-1M, but trails it by 27.3% on MovieLens-20M. On the four remaining datasets, at least one probe also outperforms our sampled-softmax SASRec reproduction by 9-28%, suggesting that these widely used benchmarks are poorly suited to measuring gains from higher-order sequence modelling. More broadly, comparing Transformer-based models against strong recency-weighted pairwise probes provides a concrete test of whether a benchmark can meaningfully measure gains from higher-order sequence modelling.

</details>


### [184] [SCoRD: Semantic-Assisted Continual Retriever-Reranker Distillation for LLM-Based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19998)
*Seunghyun Baek,Gyuseok Lee,Seunghan Lee,Wonbin Kweon,Dong Wang,SeongKu Kang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出SCoRD框架，通过语义推理助手将LLM重排器的知识蒸馏给检索器，实现非平稳数据流下的高效、低成本的检索-重排协同自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的两阶段推荐系统（ID检索器+LLM重排器）在应对不断变化的用户兴趣和稀疏数据时，频繁更新LLM并重蒸馏成本过高，而仅更新检索器因能力有限难以有效适应。

Method: 提出SCoRD（Continual Knowledge Distillation Framework），引入语义推理助手将LLM推断潜在用户意图的能力转化为可复用的意图级指导；仅在低置信度序列上选择性蒸馏重排器知识以指导检索器更新，避免重复LLM推理；并将检索器生成的表示和意图漂移信号反馈给重排器。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的实验表明，SCoRD能够实现高效且有效的检索器与重排器协同自适应。

Conclusion: SCoRD解决了非平稳数据流下LLM重排管道持续适应的高成本难题，实现了检索与重排模块的低开销协同优化。

Abstract: Recommendation systems increasingly adopt a two-stage pipeline, where an ID-based retriever retrieves candidates and an LLM-based reranker refines their rankings. To improve retrieval quality, reranker-to-retriever distillation is commonly used to transfer the reranker's knowledge to the retriever. For practical deployment, however, this pipeline must continually adapt to evolving interests and incoming interactions. A naive solution is to repeatedly update the LLM reranker and distill its latest knowledge, but this incurs prohibitive costs. Updating the retriever alone is cheaper, but its limited capacity makes adaptation from sparse data difficult. We propose SCoRD, a continual knowledge distillation framework for LLM-based reranking pipelines under a non-stationary data stream. SCoRD introduces a semantic reasoning assistant that distills the LLM's ability to infer underlying user intents into reusable intent-level guidance. It selectively distills reranker knowledge to the retriever on low-confidence sequences, guides retriever-only updates without repeated LLM inference, and feeds retriever-derived representations and intent-drift signals back to the reranker. Experiments on real-world datasets show that SCoRD enables effective and efficient retriever-reranker co-adaptation.

</details>


### [185] [Daedalus-150M: A Convolution-Attention Hybrid Designed for CPU Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20210)
*Christos Koutsiaris*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了一种专为CPU优化的混合架构小语言模型，通过固定4-bit权重和特定硬件目标反向设计网络结构。该模型在18个块中仅保留6个全注意力层，其余12个使用短卷积以消除长上下文缓存依赖。实验表明，该模型在保持竞争力的同时，显著提升了推理速度并减小了模型体积，优于多个同等规模的传统模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统小型语言模型通常先构建大型模型再进行压缩适配CPU，导致效率低下。本研究旨在通过“自下而上”的方式，针对特定用户、Token精度（4-bit）及普通CPU硬件进行逆向架构设计，以解决传统Transformer在长上下文下的内存带宽瓶颈和推理延迟问题。

Method: 1. **混合架构设计**：构建一个18层的模型，其中前6层使用标准全注意力机制（Full Attention），后12层使用宽度仅为两个时间步的短卷积（Short Convolutions），从而避免随着对话长度增加而扩大KV Cache。
2. **训练策略**：从599亿Tokens的数据集上从头训练，采用4-bit量化权重。
3. **对比实验**：与GPT-2、Pythia、OPT等相同参数量的全注意力模型以及MobileLLM进行基准测试和性能对比。

Result: 1. **性能指标**：在五任务基准测试中获得47.31分，超过预设基线42.20；验证比特率（bits-per-byte）为0.8685。
2. **优势对比**：击败了GPT-2 (124M)、Pythia-160M、OPT-125M和GPT-neo-125M（尽管它们使用了3-6倍更多的数据）；超越MobileLLM-125M的表现（尽管后者使用了万亿级数据）。
3. **效率提升**：相比同规模全注意力模型，生成的4-bit文件体积小6.3%，在2048 Tokens上下文时解码速度快1.76倍（相比外部同类模型快2.08倍）。
4. **机制验证**：速度优势随上下文长度增加而显著增长，证实了减少内存读取对长序列处理的必要性，而非单纯由模型轻量化导致。

Conclusion: 研究证明，针对特定硬件约束（如CPU内存带宽限制）进行架构定制，而非简单压缩大型模型，能显著提升小语言模型的推理效率和实用性。混合注意力-卷积架构能有效平衡表达能力与计算资源消耗，特别是在长上下文场景中表现优异。同时指出了未缓解的4-bit量化质量损失、部分卷积通道失效及词汇表过大等问题作为未来改进方向。

Abstract: Small language models are usually built like large ones and then squeezed onto a CPU afterwards. We did the opposite: we fixed the target first, one user, one token at a time, 4-bit weights, ordinary CPU, and chose the architecture to suit it. The result keeps full attention in only 6 of its 18 blocks. The other 12 use short convolutions whose memory is two timesteps wide no matter how long the conversation gets, so two thirds of the network never re-reads a growing cache.
  Trained from scratch on 59.9B tokens, the model scores 47.31 on a five-task benchmark against a bar of 42.20 that was fixed before training began. It beats GPT-2 124M, Pythia-160M, OPT-125M and GPT-neo-125M, all trained on three to six times more data, and exceeds MobileLLM-125M's published score despite that model seeing a trillion tokens. Validation bits-per-byte is 0.8685.
  To check the architecture rather than the training recipe, we trained a conventional all-attention model of the same size on the same data, and wrote down the winning condition before scoring either. The hybrid won the chosen quality metric by 0.81%, matched it on downstream tasks, produced a 6.3% smaller 4-bit file, and decoded 1.76x faster at 2048 tokens of context, 2.08x against an external model of similar size. In every measurement the speed advantage is near zero at an empty context and grows with length, which is what the mechanism predicts and what a merely leaner model would not show. A simple bandwidth calculation predicts only 1.17x, so memory volume alone does not explain the gap.
  We also report what did not work: an unmitigated 4-bit quality cost, roughly half the convolution channels ending up inert and impossible to remove, and a vocabulary larger than this model size warrants.

</details>


### [186] [What Makes a Good Fiqh Retriever? Answer Retrieval for Arabic Islamic Jurisprudence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20246)
*Somaya Eltanbouly,Heba Sbahi,Samer Rashwani,Abdessalam Bouchekif,Mutaz al-Khatib,Shahd Gaben,Mohammed Ghaly*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对伊斯兰教法（Fiqh）问答中检索与生成错误难以分离的问题，本文构建了阿拉伯语教法检索测试集，评估了多种检索策略。研究发现，针对特定教法学派（madhhab）的过滤能显著提升性能，主要挑战在于区分包含 rulings 的相关段落与仅主题相似但无答案的段落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的增强生成系统多采用端到端评估，导致检索失败和生成失败难以隔离分析。需要专门研究“携带答案的检索”（answer-bearing retrieval），即段落必须明确陈述问题所需的裁决才被视为相关。

Method: 1. 构建阿拉伯语教法（Arabic fiqh）的检索测试集。
2. 评估五种检索策略：稠密检索、词汇检索、混合检索、微调检索以及基于教法学派感知的检索。
3. 进行误差分析以识别主要难点。

Result: 1. 最佳基础检索器的 MRR@5 为 0.524。
2. 微调将性能提升至 0.553。
3. 对于强模型，混合检索带来的增益有限。
4. 在特定教法学派的问题上，引入教法学派感知过滤使 MRR@5 翻倍以上。

Conclusion: 在伊斯兰教法问答中，区分包含具体裁决的相关段落与仅主题相似但缺乏答案的段落是主要挑战；通过结合教法学派信息进行过滤可显著优化检索效果。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation is used for Islamic question answering, but most systems are evaluated end-to-end, making retrieval failures difficult to isolate from generation failures. We study answer-bearing retrieval for Arabic fiqh, where a passage is relevant only if it states the ruling required by the question. We build a retrieval test collection for Arabic fiqh and use it to evaluate dense, lexical, hybrid, fine-tuned, and madhhab-aware retrieval strategies. The best retriever achieves 0.524 MRR@5, while fine-tuning improves performance to 0.553. Hybrid retrieval provides limited gains for strong models, whereas madhhab-aware filtering more than doubles MRR@5 on school-specific questions. We further present an error analysis showing that the main challenge is distinguishing answer-bearing passages from topically similar passages that do not contain the requested ruling.

</details>


### [187] [Projecting BrowseComp-Plus onto ClimbMix: Toward More Realistic Corpora for Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20317)
*Sahel Sharifymoghaddam,Lingwei Gu,Yijun Ge,Jimmy Lin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出BrowseComp-Plus_CM基准，通过将证据从原有小规模语料库迁移至NVIDIA发布的400B-token ClimbMix大规模语料库，并构建自动化投影流水线，解决了原基准中证据与干扰项被针对查询预筛选的问题，从而更纯粹地评估智能体的检索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有的BrowseComp-Plus基准虽然分离了智能体角色与检索器角色，但其使用的语料库仅包含约10万份文档，且证据和干扰项均针对特定查询预先选择，导致评估不够真实。需要一种方法将现有问题的证据迁移到更大规模、无偏见的大语言模型预训练语料库中，以重新评估检索难度。

Method: 构建了通用的“投影流水线”（projection pipeline）：1. 将每个问题分解为原子推理步骤；2. 在新的大规模语料库ClimbMix中为每一步骤寻找支持证据；3. 通过自动验证、独立智能体评估和人工审查三重确认机制，确保每个步骤均得到支持，仅保留完全 grounded 的问题。该流水线适用于任何可分解为可验证事实的基准。

Result: 应用于830个原始测试问题后，最终获得57个完全 grounding 的问题及相关相关性判断。实验显示，在更强的智能体上，答案准确率下降5分，证据召回率从84.3%大幅降至21.4%，搜索调用次数增加63%，表明难度成功转移至检索环节。

Conclusion: 该方法有效重构了智能体搜索基准，揭示了当前智能体在大范围无偏见语料库中进行检索时的性能瓶颈。作者开源了投影流水线、新基准及分析代码，推动了更严谨的智能体评估研究。

Abstract: The BrowseComp-Plus benchmark disentangled the evaluation of agentic search by replacing opaque web search with a fixed corpus, so that an agent's role can be separated from the retriever's. That corpus, however, holds only about 100K documents and was assembled from the supporting documents of the benchmark's own queries plus mined hard negatives, so the evidence and the distractors were both selected per query. We introduce $\text{BrowseComp-Plus}_{\text{CM}}$, which keeps the BrowseComp-Plus questions but relocates their evidence to ClimbMix, a 400B-token, 553M-document mixture of web text released by NVIDIA for pre-training language models and built without reference to any benchmark. Our main contribution is the projection pipeline that makes this possible: it decomposes each question into atomic reasoning hops and grounds every hop in the new corpus, retaining a question only when automatic verification, an independent agent, and human review all confirm that every hop is supported. The pipeline is dataset-agnostic and applies to any benchmark whose questions decompose into verifiable facts. Applied to the 830 BrowseComp-Plus test questions, our pipeline yields 57 fully grounded questions with question-level relevance judgments. Projection shifts the difficulty onto retrieval, as the strongest agent we evaluate loses five points of answer accuracy but sees its evidence recall fall from 84.3% to 21.4% while issuing 63% more search calls. As the first of a series of projections, we release the pipeline, the benchmark, and our analyses at https://github.com/castorini/cmass.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [188] [Rewriting Ontology-Mediated Property Graph Queries into GQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20092)
*Bianca Löhnert,Nikolaus Augsten,Cem Okulmus,Magdalena Ortiz*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对本体中介导航图查询的实际查询重写技术，解决了理论算法长期缺乏实践支持的问题。通过结合标准化的GQL语言和DL-Lite变体本体，实现了在Cypher中评估具有嵌套双向正则路径查询的能力，并通过原型实验验证了该方法的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于本体的数据访问旨在处理图数据，但实际支持仍局限于缺乏导航和路径匹配功能的类SQL语言。虽然导航查询的理论算法早已存在，但由于实际图查询语言表达能力不足，无法支持重写常见本体语言，导致这些算法从未被实现。

Method: 利用GQL和SQL/PGQ的最新标准化成果，提出一种实用的查询重写技术。具体而言，使用针对属性图优化的DL-Lite变体编写本体，并使用包含嵌套双向正则路径查询的GQL片段（可在Cypher中评估）编写查询。

Result: 开发了一个概念验证原型，初步实验表明，结合本体知识与图数据查询的方法已具备实际可行性。

Conclusion: 随着图查询语言的标准化和表达能力的提升，本体中介的导航图查询终于变得切实可行，填补了理论与实践之间的空白。

Abstract: Ontology-based data access is intended for graph data, but practical support remains limited to SQL-like languages lacking the navigational and path-matching features fundamental to graph querying. Theoretical algorithms for navigational queries have long been available. Still, they have never been implemented, largely because practical graph query languages fell short of their theoretical counterparts and lacked the expressive power needed to support rewriting common ontology languages.
  The recent standardisation efforts around GQL and SQL/PGQ finally allow us to overcome this barrier and present a practical query rewriting technique for ontology-mediated navigational graph queries. Ontologies are written in a DL-Lite variant tuned to property graphs, and the queries in a GQL fragment with nested two-way regular path queries and which can be evaluated in Cypher. Preliminary experiments with our proof-of-concept prototype suggest that querying graph data with ontological knowledge may finally be within reach.

</details>


### [189] [Inductive Process Discovery from Partially Ordered Event Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20211)
*Humam Kourani,Tom Breuer,Gyunam Park,Wil M. P. van der Aalst*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出将归纳挖掘器（IM）从全序迹扩展到偏序迹，通过重构迹抽象层和递归投影直接处理偏序，避免线性化开销，更忠实地保留并发信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统IM技术假设迹是全序序列，这会导致系统性偏差：隐藏固有的并发关系或引入未观察到的虚假顺序依赖，特别是在活动具有持续时间、时间戳粗糙或仅约束部分事件对的情况下。现有的基于序列的抽象难以直接应用于偏序，因为线性化在高度并发场景下计算成本过高。

Method: 重新定义迹抽象层和递归投影机制，使其直接在偏序上操作，而非对偏序进行线性化。该方法保守地兼容全序迹，保留了IM原有的递归结构和正确性保证，同时避免了线性化带来的组合爆炸问题。

Result: 实验表明，该方法避免了线性化的组合开销，降低了对时间戳任意排序的敏感性，并通过在迹层面保留并发信息，能够从更少的观测数据中学习过程行为。

Conclusion: 将IM发现从全序推广到偏序是可行且高效的，它在保持算法核心优势的同时，提供了对并发过程更准确、更鲁棒的建模能力。

Abstract: The Inductive Miner (IM) family is a prominent class of process discovery techniques, combining efficient recursive decomposition with soundness-by-construction guarantees. However, IM techniques usually assume traces to be totally ordered sequences of activity occurrences. This assumption is convenient, but can introduce systematic bias: activities may have durations, events may share coarse timestamps, or the data may constrain only some event pairs. Forcing such executions into arbitrary sequences hides inherent concurrency and may introduce sequential dependencies that were never observed as causal constraints. Partial orders provide a more faithful representation, but integrating them into IM discovery is challenging because standard abstractions are sequence-based; directly reusing them would require linearizing each partial order, which becomes prohibitively expensive under high concurrency. We introduce a lifting of IM discovery from total orders to partially ordered traces. Instead of redesigning the miner and its cut detection logic, we redefine the trace abstraction layer and the recursive projections to operate directly on partial orders. The approach is conservative over totally ordered traces, avoids linearization explosion, and preserves the recursive structure and guarantees that make IM attractive. Experimental results show that the proposed lifting avoids the combinatorial overhead of linearization, reduces sensitivity to arbitrary tie-breaking in timestamped event data, and allows process behavior to be learned from fewer observations by preserving concurrency at the trace level.

</details>


### [190] [Which Eviction Policy Should an LLM Cache Use? A Systematic Study Across Workloads, Capacities, and Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20280)
*Yash Kulkarni,Shubham Harkare,Arvind Suresh Yogesh Babu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 研究评估了语义缓存中的多种驱逐策略，发现LFU表现最佳且优势微弱；同时指出当前阈值设置导致实际可替代命中率极低，建议部署前验证答案有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语义缓存驱逐政策缺乏在统一协议下的系统性比较，且对策略性能提升潜力及实际命中质量的评估不足。

Method: 使用CLEVER框架，在三个去重查询语料库、三种缓存容量和两种编码器下，评估FIFO、LRU、LFU、ARC、GDSF、SISO流式适配版及语义冗余策略的性能。

Result: 1. 没有任何策略在18种设置中比LFU提升超过0.041个百分点；FIFO和流式SISO在紧凑容量下落后LFU约8.6个百分点。2. 几何分析显示新插入条目难以获得冗余信号，解释了复杂策略无效的原因。3. 审计发现仅1.1-2.2%的原始命中是答案可替代的，质量调整后命中率大幅降低。4. 不同嵌入模型的阈值不具备可迁移性。

Conclusion: LFU是该协议下最强的简单默认策略。部署决策应首先确立答案的有效性（answer validity），随后通过精确搜索测试子点策略差异，而非盲目依赖复杂的驱逐算法。

Abstract: Semantic caches reuse an LLM response when the incoming query embedding lies near a cached query, but proposed eviction policies have rarely been compared under one protocol. Using CLEVER, we evaluate FIFO, LRU, LFU, ARC, GDSF, a single-pass streaming adaptation of SISO, and a semantic-redundancy policy across three ordered, deduplicated query corpora, three cache capacities, and two encoders. No evaluated policy improves on LFU by more than 0.041 percentage points in any of the eighteen settings. Replacement is not irrelevant: FIFO and streaming SISO trail LFU by as much as 8.67 and 8.55 points, respectively, at tight capacity.
  We explain the missing upside with a conditional packing result. Under exact lookup and insert-on-miss, a newly inserted entry cannot have a resident neighbor within the hit radius, so a geometry-aware eviction rule receives little new redundancy signal. A separate audit exposes a larger problem with the evaluated operating point. At MiniLM's median nearest-neighbor threshold, only 2.1-3.9% of sampled LMSYS and QQP hits are judged answer-substitutable, reducing raw hit rates of 51-60% to quality-adjusted rates of 1.1-2.2%. The cross-encoder study further shows that thresholds do not transfer between embedding models. LFU is the strongest simple default in this protocol; deployment decisions should first establish answer validity and then test sub-point policy differences with exact search.

</details>
